nt } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',\n  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',\n};\n\n// 需清除的业务数据表（均 ON DELETE CASCADE，逐表删除即可，顺序不影响结果）\nconst BUSINESS_TABLES = [\n  'order_status_logs',\n  'withdrawal_review_logs',\n  'withdrawal_requests',\n  'voucher_redeem_requests',\n  'exchange_orders',\n  'notifications',\n  'rush_early_access',\n  'user_coupons',\n  'user_addresses',\n  'transfer_records',\n  'team_splits',\n  'order_splits',\n  'elimination_records',\n  'screening_records',\n  'daily_screenings',\n  'user_assessments',\n  'kyc_applications',\n  'distribution_relations',\n  'morning_reward_records',\n  'referral_rewards',\n  'commission_records',\n  'account_transactions',\n  'virtual_accounts',\n  'payment_accounts',\n  'leader_qualification_reviews',\n  'mobile_sessions',\n  'products',\n  'orders',\n];\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });\n\n  try {\n    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;\n    const serviceKey = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!;\n    const db = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, {\n      auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },\n    });\n\n    // 鉴权：仅超级管理员（admin_users.role = 'super_admin'）\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization') ?? 0;\n    }\n    const { count: nonBossAfter } = await db.from('users').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_super_admin', false);\n    after['users(非老板)'] = nonBossAfter ?? 0;\n    const { count: bossCount } = await db.from('users').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_super_admin', true);\n\n    const summary = Object.keys(before)\n      .filter(k => before[k] > 0)\n      .map(k => ({ table: k, cleared: before[k] - (after[k] ?? 0) }));\n\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({\n      success: true,\n      boss_preserved: bossCount ?? 0,\n      summary,\n      total_cleared: summary.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cleared, 0),\n    }), {\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(e) }), {\n      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    });\n  }\n});"}]}

--BEGIN AGENT OBSERVATION--
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import type { Session, User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { AdminRole } from '@/lib/roles';

export interface AdminProfile {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  display_name: string;
  role: AdminRole;
  is_active: boolean;
}

interface AuthContextValue {
  session: Session | null;
  user: User | null;
  adminProfile: AdminProfile | null;
  loading: boolean;
  signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: string | null }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

async function fetchAdminProfile(userId: string): Promise<AdminProfile | null> {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('admin_profiles')
    .select('id, email, display_name, role, is_active')
    .eq('id', userId)
    .maybeSingle();
  return data ?? null;
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [adminProfile, setAdminProfile] = useState<AdminProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const loadProfile = async (sess: Session | null) => {
    if (sess?.user) {
      const profile = await fetchAdminProfile(sess.user.id);
      setAdminProfile(profile);
    } else {
      setAdminProfile(null);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(async ({ data: { session } }) => {
      setSession(session);
      await loadProfile(session);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
      await loadProfile(session);
    });

    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) return { error: error.message };
    // 登录后检查是否为有效管理员
    const profile = data.user ? await fetchAdminProfile(data.user.id) : null;
    if (!profile) {
      await supabase.auth.signOut();
      return { error: '账号不存在或无管理员权限' };
    }
    if (!profile.is_active) {
      await supabase.auth.signOut();
      return { error: '账号已被禁用，请联系超级管理员' };
    }
    setAdminProfile(profile);
    return { error: null };
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setAdminProfile(null);
  };

  const refreshProfile = async () => {
    if (session?.user) {
      const profile = await fetchAdminProfile(session.user.id);
      setAdminProfile(profile);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ session, user: session?.user ?? null, adminProfile, loading, signIn, signOut, refreshProfile }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {

```
-- ============================================================================
-- 数据清理脚本：清除「订单列表」相关的所有测试数据
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 清理范围：
--   1. 订单主表及订单状态日志、拆单记录、转拍/赠送记录
--   2. 订单关联的奖励/分润记录（推荐奖励、佣金、早市激励奖励）
--   3. 支付与资金记录（提现申请及审核日志、收款账户绑定、账户流水）
--   4. 重置非老板用户的虚拟账户余额（清除订单产生的资金）
-- 保留范围（生产数据）：
--   - 老板账号(is_super_admin=true)及其虚拟账户、权限配置
--   - 系统配置、商品分类、抢购时段、激励配置、活动/公告/Banner 等运营配置
--   - 数据库表结构与核心业务逻辑代码
--
-- 说明：所有外键均为 ON DELETE CASCADE，按依赖顺序删除以确保安全。
--       脚本包裹在事务中，任一步骤失败将自动回滚，不影响生产数据。
-- 用法：在 Supabase SQL Editor 或 psql 中执行本脚本。
-- ============================================================================

BEGIN;

-- ① 订单关联子表（先于主表删除，避免外键约束冲突）
DELETE FROM public.order_status_logs;        -- 订单状态流转日志
DELETE FROM public.order_splits;             -- 拆单记录
DELETE FROM public.transfer_records;         -- 转拍/赠送记录

-- ② 订单关联的奖励与分润记录
DELETE FROM public.referral_rewards;         -- 推荐奖励（关联订单）
DELETE FROM public.commission_records;       -- 佣金/分润记录（关联订单）
DELETE FROM public.morning_reward_records;   -- 早市激励奖励（关联订单）

-- ③ 支付与资金记录
DELETE FROM public.withdrawal_review_logs;   -- 提现审核日志
DELETE FROM public.withdrawal_requests;      -- 提现申请
DELETE FROM public.payment_accounts;         -- 收款账户绑定
DELETE FROM public.account_transactions;     -- 账户资金流水

-- ④ 重置非老板用户的虚拟账户余额（清除订单产生的资金，保留老板账户）
UPDATE public.virtual_accounts
SET balance = 0,
    frozen_balance = 0,
    total_in = 0
WHERE user_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.users WHERE is_super_admin = false);

-- ⑤ 订单主表（最后删除，CASCADE 会再次兜底清理任何遗漏的关联数据）
DELETE FROM public.orders;

-- ⑥ 重置自增/序列（如存在），确保新订单编号从干净起点开始
-- 注：orders 主键为 UUID，无需重置序列；如使用 bigint 自增可取消下面注释
-- SELECT setval(pg_get_serial_sequence('public.orders','id'), 1, false);

COMMIT;

-- 校验：执行后可运行以下查询确认订单相关数据已清空
-- SELECT
--   (SELECT count(*) FROM public.orders)               AS orders,
--   (SELECT count(*) FROM public.order_status_logs)    AS status_logs,
--   (SELECT count(*) FROM public.referral_rewards)     AS referral_rewards,
--   (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_transactions) AS transactions,
--   (SELECT count(*) FROM public.users WHERE is_super_admin = true) AS boss_preserved;
```

     1	/**
     2	 * 交易结算工具
     3	 * 卖方确认收款后：
     4	 *  1. 结算卖方全额入账（balance 账户）
     5	 *  2. 按系统分润费率分配奖励：
     6	 *     - 商家分红（merchant_bonus_rate）→ 买方 bonus 账户
     7	 *     - 老板分红（boss_bonus_rate）    → 固定老板用户 bonus 账户
     8	 *     - 代金券储备（voucher_reserve_rate 0.1%）→ 买方 points 账户 + 全局 voucher_pool 累计
     9	 *     - 推广奖金（direct_referral_rate 0.2%）→ 买方直接推荐人 promotion 账户
    10	 *  3. 推荐奖励（referral_ancestor_rate 订单金额 × 0.2%）：
    11	 *     向上遍历推荐链，找到「当天中午12点前完成抢购订单」的最近推荐人作为奖励获得者；
    12	 *     若整条链都未在截止前完成，则奖励给最上级推荐人（链顶兜底）
    13	 */
    14	import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
    15	
    16	interface SettleParams {
    17	  orderId: string;
    18	  sellerId: string;
    19	  buyerId: string;
    20	  /** 订单成交金额 */
    21	  orderAmount: number;
    22	  /** 已废弃，保留兼容 */
    23	  consignmentFee?: number;
    24	  storageFee?: number;
    25	}
    26	
    27	export interface SettleResult {
    28	  success: boolean;
    29	  netAmount: number;
    30	  serviceFee: number;
    31	  error?: string;
    32	}
    33	
    34	/** 安全增加账户余额（直接操作底层 virtual_accounts 表，绕过视图写限制） */
    35	async function addUserAccount(
    36	  userId: string,
    37	  accountType: string,
    38	  amount: number,
    39	  orderId: string,
    40	  desc: string,
    41	) {
    42	  if (amount <= 0) return;
    43	  // 查底层表（user_accounts 是视图，INSERT 不可用）
    44	  const { data: acct } = await supabase
    45	    .from('virtual_accounts')
    46	    .select('id,balance,total_in')
    47	    .eq('user_id', userId)
    48	    .eq('account_type', accountType)
    49	    .maybeSingle();
    50	
    51	  let accountId: string;
    52	  let balanceAfter: number;
    53	
    54	  if (acct) {
    55	    const newBalance = Number((Number(acct.balance) + amount).toFixed(2));
    56	    const newTotalIn = Number((Number(acct.total_in ?? 0) + amount).toFixed(2));
    57	    await supabase.from('virtual_accounts').update({
    58	      balance: newBalance,
    59	      total_in: newTotalIn,
    60	      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    61	    }).eq('id', acct.id);
    62	    accountId   = acct.id;
    63	    balanceAfter = newBalance;
    64	  } else {
    65	    const { data: newAcct } = await supabase.from('virtual_accounts').insert({
    66	      user_id:      userId,
    67	      account_type: accountType,
    68	      balance:      amount,
    69	      total_in:     amount,
    70	      total_out:    0,
    71	      updated_at:   new Date().toISOString(),
    72	    }).select('id').single();
    73	    if (!newAcct) return;
    74	    accountId   = newAcct.id;
    75	    balanceAfter = amount;
    76	  }
    77	
    78	  await supabase.from('account_transactions').insert({
    79	    account_id:       accountId,
    80	    user_id:          userId,
    81	    account_type:     accountType,
    82	    type:             'in',
    83	    amount,
    84	    balance_after:    balanceAfter,
    85	    related_order_id: orderId,
    86	    description:      desc,
    87	  });
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * 结算卖家收益 + 分润分配
    92	 */
    93	export async function settleSellerEarnings(params: SettleParams): Promise<SettleResult> {
    94	  const { orderId, sellerId, buyerId, orderAmount } = params;
    95	  const netAmount = Number(orderAmount.toFixed(2));
    96	
    97	  // ── 1. 卖方全额入账（余额账户）──
    98	  await addUserAccount(
    99	    sellerId, 'balance', netAmount, orderId,
   100	    `交易结算入账`,
   101	  );
   102	
   103	  // ── 2. 更新订单结算字段 ──
   104	  await supabase.from('orders').update({
   105	    settled_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   106	    service_fee: 0,
   107	    net_amount: netAmount,
   108	  }).eq('id', orderId);
   109	
   110	  // ── 3. 读取分润费率配置 ──
   111	  const RATE_KEYS = ['merchant_bonus_rate', 'boss_bonus_rate', 'voucher_reserve_rate'];
   112	  const { data: rateRows } = await supabase.from('system_settings').select('key,value').in('key', RATE_KEYS);
   113	  const rateMap: Record<string, number> = {};
   114	  (rateRows ?? []).forEach(r => { rateMap[r.key] = parseFloat(r.value) || 0; });
   115	  const merchantRate = rateMap['merchant_bonus_rate']  ?? 0.01;
   116	  const bossRate     = rateMap['boss_bonus_rate']      ?? 0.017;
   117	  const voucherRate  = rateMap['voucher_reserve_rate'] ?? 0.001;
   118	  // 注意：direct_referral_rate（旧推广奖金）已废弃，推荐奖励统一由 Step 7 settleReferralReward 处理
   119	
   120	  // ── 4. 老板分红固定账户 ──
   121	  const BOSS_USER_ID = 'a256890e-d87a-4b90-8158-301007001c23'; // 13924151349
   122	
   123	  // ── 5. 并行分发三笔奖励（已移除旧推广奖金，避免与递推推荐奖励重复发放） ──
   124	  const merchantAmt = Number((orderAmount * merchantRate).toFixed(2));
   125	  const bossAmt     = Number((orderAmount * bossRate).toFixed(2));
   126	  const voucherAmt  = Number((orderAmount * voucherRate).toFixed(2));
   127	
   128	  await Promise.all([
   129	    // 商家分红 → 买方 bonus
   130	    addUserAccount(buyerId, 'bonus', merchantAmt, orderId,
   131	      `商家分红（${(merchantRate * 100).toFixed(1)}%）`),
   132	    // 老板分红 → 固定老板账户 bonus
   133	    addUserAccount(BOSS_USER_ID, 'bonus', bossAmt, orderId,
   134	      `老板分红（${(bossRate * 100).toFixed(1)}%）`),
   135	    // 代金券储备 0.1% → 买方 points 账户（累计到门槛可兑换）+ 全局资金池
   136	    (async () => {
   137	      await addUserAccount(buyerId, 'points', voucherAmt, orderId,
   138	        `代金券储备（${(voucherRate * 100).toFixed(1)}%）`);
   139	      // 同步更新全局平台代金券资金池
   140	      const { data: pool } = await supabase.from('voucher_pool').select('id,accumulated').order('id').limit(1).maybeSingle();
   141	      if (pool) {
   142	        await supabase.from('voucher_pool').update({
   143	          accumulated: Number((Number(pool.accumulated) + voucherAmt).toFixed(4)),
   144	          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   145	        }).eq('id', pool.id);
   146	      }
   147	    })(),
   148	  ]);
   149	
   150	  // ── 6. 体验商家首单完成 → 自动升级为正式商家 ──
   151	  const { data: buyerInfo } = await supabase
   152	    .from('users')
   153	    .select('merchant_type')
   154	    .eq('id', buyerId)
   155	    .maybeSingle();
   156	  if (buyerInfo?.merchant_type === 'trial') {
   157	    await supabase.from('users').update({
   158	      merchant_type: 'regular',
   159	      promoted_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   160	    }).eq('id', buyerId);
   161	  }
   162	
   163	  // ── 7. 推荐奖励：向上遍历链，奖励第一个「已买单」的祖先（订单金额 × 0.2%）──
   164	  await settleReferralReward({ orderId, buyerId, orderAmount });
   165	
   166	  return { success: true, netAmount, serviceFee: 0 };
   167	}
   168	
   169	/**
   170	 * 向上遍历推荐链，按「当天中午12点前完成抢购订单」规则递推发放推荐奖励。
   171	 *
   172	 * 规则（订单金额 × referral_ancestor_rate，默认 0.2%，发到 promotion 账户）：
   173	 *  - 「当天」= 被推荐人本次结算触发时的北京日期
   174	 *  - 「完成抢购订单」= 该推荐人作为买家、在当天中午12:00前有 confirmed 状态的抢购订单(is_rush=true)
   175	 *  - 从直接推荐人开始逐级向上，找到最近的达标推荐人即奖励给他，并记录被跳过的中间层
   176	 *  - 若整条链都未在截止时间前完成订单 → 奖励给最上级推荐人（链顶）
   177	 *  - 若买方无任何推荐人 → 不发放
   178	 *  - 幂等：referral_rewards 表 order_id 唯一检查，防止重复发放
   179	 */
   180	async function settleReferralReward({
   181	  orderId,
   182	  buyerId,
   183	  orderAmount,
   184	}: {
   185	  orderId: string;
   186	  buyerId: string;
   187	  orderAmount: number;
   188	}) {
   189	  // 幂等检查：已存在则直接跳过
   190	  const { count: already } = await supabase
   191	    .from('referral_rewards')
   192	    .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   193	    .eq('order_id', orderId);
   194	  if ((already ?? 0) > 0) return;
   195	
   196	  // 读取奖励比例配置（默认 0.2%）
   197	  const { data: cfg } = await supabase
   198	    .from('system_settings')
   199	    .select('value')
   200	    .eq('key', 'referral_ancestor_rate')
   201	    .maybeSingle();
   202	  const ancestorRate = parseFloat(cfg?.value ?? '0.002');
   203	  const rewardAmt = Number((orderAmount * ancestorRate).toFixed(2));
   204	  if (rewardAmt <= 0) return;
   205	
   206	  // 「当天」= 结算触发时的北京日期；截止时间 = 当天中午12:00（北京时间）
   207	  const bjNow = new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 3600000);
   208	  const bjTodayStr = bjNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // YYYY-MM-DD（北京时间）
   209	  const deadlineUtc = new Date(`${bjTodayStr}T12:00:00+08:00`).toISOString();
   210	
   211	  // 向上遍历推荐链（最多10层防止死循环）
   212	  const MAX_DEPTH = 10;
   213	  let currentId = buyerId;
   214	  const skippedIds: string[] = [];
   215	  let topReferrerId: string | null = null; // 记录链顶推荐人，用于兜底
   216	
   217	  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_DEPTH; i++) {
   218	    const { data: row } = await supabase
   219	      .from('users')
   220	      .select('referrer_id')
   221	      .eq('id', currentId)
   222	      .maybeSingle();
   223	    const referrerId = row?.referrer_id;
   224	    if (!referrerId) break; // 已到链顶
   225	
   226	    topReferrerId = referrerId; // 持续更新为最上级推荐人
   227	
   228	    // 检查该推荐人是否在「当天中午12:00前」完成过抢购订单（confirmed + is_rush）
   229	    const { count: rushCnt } = await supabase
   230	      .from('orders')
   231	      .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   232	      .eq('buyer_id', referrerId)
   233	      .eq('is_rush', true)
   234	      .eq('status', 'confirmed')
   235	      .lt('created_at', deadlineUtc);
   236	
   237	    if ((rushCnt ?? 0) > 0) {
   238	      // 找到达标推荐人，发放奖励
   239	      await addUserAccount(
   240	        referrerId, 'promotion', rewardAmt, orderId,
   241	        `推荐奖励（当天12点前完成抢购）`,
   242	      );
   243	      await supabase.from('referral_rewards').insert({
   244	        order_id:     orderId,
   245	        buyer_id:     buyerId,
   246	        recipient_id: referrerId,
   247	        skipped_ids:  skippedIds,
   248	        amount:       rewardAmt,
   249	        status:       'settled',
   250	      });
   251	      console.log(`[settlement] referral_reward: buyer=${buyerId} → recipient=${referrerId}(达标), skipped=${skippedIds.length}, amount=${rewardAmt}`);
   252	      return;
   253	    }
   254	
   255	    // 未达标，写一条提醒记录通知该推荐人（金额为0，仅作提醒），继续向上
   256	    await supabase.from('account_transactions').insert({
   257	      user_id:          referrerId,
   258	      account_type:     'promotion',
   259	      type:             'notice',
   260	      amount:           0,
   261	      related_order_id: orderId,
   262	      description:      `推荐奖励提醒：您未在当天12点前完成抢购，本次推荐奖励已转给上级推荐人，请明日12点前完成抢购`,
   263	      created_at:       new Date().toISOString(),
   264	    });
   265	    skippedIds.push(referrerId);
   266	    currentId = referrerId;
   267	  }
   268	
   269	  // 整条链都未在截止时间前完成订单 → 奖励给最上级推荐人
   270	  if (topReferrerId) {
   271	    await addUserAccount(
   272	      topReferrerId, 'promotion', rewardAmt, orderId,
   273	      `推荐奖励（链顶兜底）`,
   274	    );
   275	    await supabase.from('referral_rewards').insert({
   276	      order_id:     orderId,
   277	      buyer_id:     buyerId,
   278	      recipient_id: topReferrerId,
   279	      skipped_ids:  skippedIds,
   280	      amount:       rewardAmt,
   281	      status:       'settled',
   282	    });
   283	    console.log(`[settlement] referral_reward: buyer=${buyerId} → top=${topReferrerId}(链顶兜底), skipped=${skippedIds.length}, amount=${rewardAmt}`);
   284	  } else {
   285	    console.log(`[settlement] referral_reward: no referrer for buyer=${buyerId}`);
   286	  }
   287	}
[The command completed with exit code 0.]

   366                     {r.completed_at ? new Date(r.completed_at).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN') : '-'}
   367                   </td>
   368                 </tr>
   369               );
   370             })}
   371           </tbody>
   372         </table>
   373       </div>
   374 
   375       {/* 分页 */}
   376       {totalPages > 1 && (
   377         <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   378           <span>共 {total} 条，第 {page}/{totalPages} 页</span>
   379           <div className="flex gap-1">
   380             <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setPage(p => Math.max(1, p - 1))} disabled={page === 1}
   381               className="h-7 px-3 text-xs border border-border">上一页</Button>
   382             <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setPage(p => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))} disabled={page === totalPages}
   383               className="h-7 px-3 text-xs border border-border">下一页</Button>
   384           </div>
   385         </div>
   386       )}
   387     </AdminLayout>
   388   );
   389 }


</observation>

AgentFinishAction(final_thought='# 买单仓库 `removeChild` 错误已修复 ✅\n\n**根本原因：**\n\nRadix UI Tabs 会将**所有** `TabsContent` 同时挂载到 DOM（通过 CSS 隐藏非活动 tab）。而原代码在组件顶层只算了一次 `filtered`（依赖当前 `tab` 状态），6 个 TabsContent 全部使用这同一份列表。\n\n切换 tab 时，`filtered` 变化，导致**同一批订单 DOM 节点**（含 `<input type="file">` 及 ref 回调）在 6 个 TabsContent 里同时被创建/销毁。React reconcile 时找不到预期父节点，抛出 `removeChild: The node to be removed is not a child of this node`。\n\n**修复：** 将 `filtered` 的计算移入每个 `TabsContent` 内部，每个 tab 独立按自己的 `t.value` 过滤订单，各自维护独立的 DOM 节点，彻底消除节点归属冲突。\n\n```questions\n所有tab懒加载：将TabsContent改为只挂载当前激活的tab，减少不必要的DOM渲染\n转拍开关：在系统设置中添加手动开关，允许管理员随时开放/关闭转拍功能\n订单详情页：点击订单卡片跳转到完整订单详情页，展示所有状态流转记录\n```\n', commit_message='买单仓库 `removeChild` 错误已修复 ✅', task_completed='true', outputs={'status': 'success', 'behavior': 'code_tool', 'suggested_questions': [{'title': '所有tab懒加载', 'query': '将TabsContent改为只挂载当前激活的tab，减少不必要的DOM渲染'}, {'title': '转拍开关', 'query': '在系统设置中添加手动开关，允许管理员随时开放/关闭转拍功能'}, {'title': '订单详情页', 'query': '点击订单卡片跳转到完整订单详情页，展示所有状态流转记录'}], 'type': 'standard_script', 'finish_type': 'end_code', 'issue': '买单仓库 `removeChild` 错误已修复 ✅', 'button_actions': [{'type': 'button', 'event': {'args': {'versionId': 'app_version-dommaasvtxxc', 'versionDesc': '买单仓库 \\`removeChild\\` 错误已修复 ✅', 'versionLabel': 'v403'}, 'name': 'rollback'}, 'label': '恢复', 'value': '恢复'}], 'app_id': 'app-cj6aqssgkd8h', 'version_id': 'app_version-dommaasvtxxc', 'user_id': 'user-amxnzpnzj20w'}, thought='', action=<ActionType.FINISH: 'finish'>)

     1	// auto-rush-order: 每天 09:29 北京时间自动为所有正式商家批量创建抢购订单
     2	//
     3	// 业务规则：
     4	//   - 仅工作日（周一～周五，北京时间）执行
     5	//   - 仅处理 merchant_type='regular' 且未被封禁的正式商家
     6	//   - 每位正式商家每天自动创建 earlyMax（默认2）单
     7	//   - 商品来源：其他卖家的已审核、活跃商品（approved + is_active=true，seller_id != 自己）
     8	//   - 幂等：今日已抢单数 >= earlyMax 则跳过
     9	//   - 商品购买后标记 is_active=false，防止重复抢购
    10	//   - 可通过 POST body { triggered_by: "manual" } 手动触发（跳过工作日校验）
    11	
    12	import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
    13	
    14	const corsHeaders = {
    15	  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
    16	  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
    17	};
    18	
    19	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    20	  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    21	
    22	  try {
    23	    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;
    24	    const serviceKey  = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!;
    25	    const db = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, {
    26	      auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    27	    });
    28	
    29	    const body = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};
    30	    const triggeredBy: string = body.triggered_by ?? 'cron';
    31	    const forceRun = triggeredBy === 'manual'; // 手动触发时跳过工作日检查
    32	
    33	    // ── 北京时间计算 ──
    34	    const nowUtc = new Date();
    35	    const bjNow  = new Date(nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000); // UTC+8
    36	    const bjDow  = bjNow.getUTCDay(); // 0=周日, 1=周一, …, 6=周六
    37	
    38	    // 工作日检查（手动触发时跳过）
    39	    if (!forceRun && (bjDow === 0 || bjDow === 6)) {
    40	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'weekend' }), {
    41	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    42	      });
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    // 今日北京日期（用于幂等检查）
    46	    const bjTodayStr = bjNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // "YYYY-MM-DD"
    47	    // 今日北京 0 点对应的 UTC 时间
    48	    const bjTodayStartUtc = new Date(
    49	      Math.floor((nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000
    50	    ).toISOString();
    51	
    52	    // 正式商家早场固定额度
    53	    const earlyMax = 2;
    54	
    55	    // ── 解析当前生效的「早场」时段：自定义活动优先 > 默认时段 ──
    56	    const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
    57	    const { data: activities } = await db
    58	      .from('rush_activities')
    59	      .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
    60	      .eq('is_active', true)
    61	      .eq('session_type', 'early')
    62	      .eq('activity_date', bjTodayStr)
    63	      .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    64	    const customEarly = (activities ?? []).find(a => nowMins >= a.start_minute && nowMins < a.end_minute);
    65	
    66	    let earlyPeriod: { id: string; isCustom: boolean } | null = null;
    67	    if (customEarly) {
    68	      earlyPeriod = { id: customEarly.id, isCustom: true };
    69	    } else {
    70	      const { data: earlySlots } = await db
    71	        .from('rush_time_slots')
    72	        .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
    73	        .eq('is_active', true)
    74	        .eq('session_type', 'early')
    75	        .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    76	      const def = (earlySlots ?? []).find(s => nowMins >= s.start_minute && nowMins < s.end_minute);
    77	      if (def) earlyPeriod = { id: def.id, isCustom: false };
    78	    }
    79	    if (!earlyPeriod) {
    80	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_in_early_session', bj_date: bjTodayStr }), {
    81	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    82	      });
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    // ── 查询所有正式商家（未封禁、已激活） ──
    86	    const { data: regularMerchants, error: merchantErr } = await db
    87	      .from('users')
    88	      .select('id, nickname, phone')
    89	      .eq('merchant_type', 'regular')
    90	      .eq('is_super_admin', false)
    91	      .eq('is_banned', false)
    92	      .eq('user_status', 'active');
    93	
    94	    if (merchantErr) throw new Error(`查询正式商家失败: ${merchantErr.message}`);
    95	
    96	    const merchants = regularMerchants ?? [];
    97	
    98	    // ── 预先拉取所有可用商品池（approved + is_active=true），先进先出分配 ──
    99	    const { data: productPool } = await db
   100	      .from('products')
   101	      .select('id, seller_id, consignment_price, title')
   102	      .eq('status', 'approved')
   103	      .eq('is_active', true)
   104	      .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
   105	
   106	    const availablePool: { id: string; seller_id: string | null; consignment_price: number; title: string }[]
   107	      = [...(productPool ?? [])];
   108	
   109	    const log: string[] = [];
   110	    let totalCreated = 0;
   111	    let totalSkipped = 0;
   112	
   113	    for (const merchant of merchants) {
   114	      // ── 幂等：查今日已创建的抢购单数 ──
   115	      const { count: todayCnt } = await db
   116	        .from('orders')
   117	        .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   118	        .eq('buyer_id', merchant.id)
   119	        .eq('is_rush', true)
   120	        .gte('created_at', bjTodayStartUtc);
   121	
   122	      const alreadyHas = todayCnt ?? 0;
   123	      const toCreate   = earlyMax - alreadyHas; // 还需创建的订单数
   124	
   125	      if (toCreate <= 0) {
   126	        totalSkipped++;
   127	        log.push(`skip:${merchant.phone}(已有${alreadyHas}单)`);
   128	        continue;
   129	      }
   130	
   131	      // ── 从池中取可用商品（排除自己作为 seller 的商品）──
   132	      const picked: typeof availablePool = [];
   133	      for (let i = 0; i < availablePool.length && picked.length < toCreate; i++) {
   134	        if (availablePool[i].seller_id !== merchant.id) {
   135	          picked.push(availablePool[i]);
   136	        }
   137	      }
   138	
   139	      if (picked.length === 0) {
   140	        log.push(`no_product:${merchant.phone}(商品池已空)`);
   141	        continue;
   142	      }
   143	
   144	      // ── 逐个商品创建抢购订单 ──
   145	      let created = 0;
   146	      for (const prod of picked) {
   147	        const orderNo = `ORD${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
   148	        const { error: insertErr } = await db.from('orders').insert({
   149	          buyer_id:   merchant.id,
   150	          seller_id:  prod.seller_id,
   151	          product_id: prod.id,
   152	          amount:     prod.consignment_price,
   153	          status:     'pending_payment',
   154	          order_no:   orderNo,
   155	          is_rush:    true,
   156	          rush_slot_id:    earlyPeriod.isCustom ? null : earlyPeriod.id,
   157	          rush_activity_id: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? earlyPeriod.id : null,
   158	        });
   159	
   160	        if (insertErr) {
   161	          log.push(`order_fail:${merchant.phone} prod=${prod.id} err=${insertErr.message}`);
   162	          continue;
   163	        }
   164	
   165	        // 标记商品不再活跃（防止重复抢购）
   166	        await db.from('products')
   167	          .update({ is_active: false })
   168	          .eq('id', prod.id);
   169	
   170	        // 从本地池中移除已分配商品（防止同批次重复分配）
   171	        const idx = availablePool.findIndex(p => p.id === prod.id);
   172	        if (idx !== -1) availablePool.splice(idx, 1);
   173	
   174	        // 清除商家"未抢"标记
   175	        await db.from('users')
   176	          .update({ rush_skipped_today: false })
   177	          .eq('id', merchant.id);
   178	
   179	        created++;
   180	        totalCreated++;
   181	        console.log(`[auto-rush-order] ✅ ${merchant.phone} 自动抢购 "${prod.title}" 订单=${orderNo}`);
   182	      }
   183	
   184	      log.push(`ok:${merchant.phone}(新建${created}单)`);
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    const summary = {
   188	      triggered_by: triggeredBy,
   189	      bj_date:      bjTodayStr,
   190	      merchants:    merchants.length,
   191	      orders_created: totalCreated,
   192	      skipped:      totalSkipped,
   193	      pool_remaining: availablePool.length,
   194	      early_max:    earlyMax,
   195	      log,
   196	    };
   197	
   198	    console.log(`[auto-rush-order] 完成 | ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
   199	
   200	    return new Response(JSON.stringify(summary), {
   201	      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   202	    });
   203	
   204	  } catch (err) {
   205	    console.error('[auto-rush-order]', err);
   206	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {
   207	      status: 500,
   208	      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   209	    });
   210	  }
   211	});
[The command completed with exit code 0.]

     1	// auto-rush-early: 正式商家进入早场时，系统自动为其完成最多 2 单抢购（按需触发，幂等）
     2	//
     3	// 业务规则：
     4	//   - 仅处理 merchant_type='regular' 且未被封禁、已激活的正式商家
     5	//   - 仅在当前北京时间命中「早场」(session_type='early') 时段时执行
     6	//   - 每位正式商家早场最多自动创建 2 单（早场固定额度）
     7	//   - 幂等：今日已抢单数 >= 2 则跳过
     8	//   - 商品来源：其他卖家的已审核、活跃商品（approved + is_active=true，seller_id != 自己）
     9	//   - 商品购买后标记 is_active=false，防止重复抢购
    10	
    11	import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';
    12	
    13	const corsHeaders = {
    14	  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
    15	  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
    16	};
    17	
    18	// 正式商家早市限购数量从 morning_incentive_config.regular_first_order_limit 读取（默认 2）
    19	
    20	Deno.serve(async (req) => {
    21	  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    22	
    23	  try {
    24	    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;
    25	    const serviceKey  = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!;
    26	    const db = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, {
    27	      auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    28	    });
    29	
    30	    const body = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};
    31	    const userId: string | undefined = body.user_id;
    32	
    33	    if (!userId) {
    34	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: '缺少 user_id' }), {
    35	        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    36	      });
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    // ── 北京时间计算 ──
    40	    const nowUtc = new Date();
    41	    const bjNow  = new Date(nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000);
    42	    const bjDow  = bjNow.getUTCDay();
    43	    const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
    44	    const bjTodayStartUtc = new Date(
    45	      Math.floor((nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000
    46	    ).toISOString();
    47	    const bjTodayStr = bjNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // "YYYY-MM-DD"（北京时间今日）
    48	
    49	    // 工作日检查
    50	    if (bjDow === 0 || bjDow === 6) {
    51	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'weekend', created: 0 }), {
    52	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    53	      });
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    // ── 校验用户为正式商家 ──
    57	    const { data: user, error: userErr } = await db
    58	      .from('users')
    59	      .select('id, merchant_type, is_banned, user_status')
    60	      .eq('id', userId)
    61	      .maybeSingle();
    62	
    63	    if (userErr) throw new Error(`查询用户失败: ${userErr.message}`);
    64	    if (!user || user.merchant_type !== 'regular' || user.is_banned || user.user_status !== 'active') {
    65	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_regular', created: 0 }), {
    66	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    67	      });
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    // ── 解析当前生效的「早场」时段：自定义活动优先 > 默认时段 ──
    71	    const { data: activities } = await db
    72	      .from('rush_activities')
    73	      .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
    74	      .eq('is_active', true)
    75	      .eq('session_type', 'early')
    76	      .eq('activity_date', bjTodayStr)
    77	      .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    78	    const customEarly = (activities ?? []).find(a => nowMins >= a.start_minute && nowMins < a.end_minute);
    79	
    80	    let earlyPeriod: { id: string; isCustom: boolean } | null = null;
    81	    if (customEarly) {
    82	      earlyPeriod = { id: customEarly.id, isCustom: true };
    83	    } else {
    84	      const { data: slots } = await db
    85	        .from('rush_time_slots')
    86	        .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
    87	        .eq('is_active', true)
    88	        .eq('session_type', 'early')
    89	        .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    90	      const def = (slots ?? []).find(s => nowMins >= s.start_minute && nowMins < s.end_minute);
    91	      if (def) earlyPeriod = { id: def.id, isCustom: false };
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    if (!earlyPeriod) {
    95	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_in_early_session', created: 0 }), {
    96	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    97	      });
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    // ── 幂等：今日已抢单数 ──
   101	    const { count: todayCnt } = await db
   102	      .from('orders')
   103	      .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   104	      .eq('buyer_id', userId)
   105	      .eq('is_rush', true)
   106	      .gte('created_at', bjTodayStartUtc);
   107	
   108	    const alreadyHas = todayCnt ?? 0;
   109	    // 正式商家早市限购数量（来自早市激励配置，默认 2）
   110	    const { data: incentiveCfg } = await db
   111	      .from('morning_incentive_config')
   112	      .select('regular_first_order_limit')
   113	      .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
   114	      .limit(1)
   115	      .maybeSingle();
   116	    const earlyMax = incentiveCfg?.regular_first_order_limit ?? 2;
   117	    const toCreate = Math.max(0, earlyMax - alreadyHas);
   118	    if (toCreate <= 0) {
   119	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'already_filled', created: 0 }), {
   120	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   121	      });
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    // ── 从商品池取可用商品（排除自己作为 seller）──
   125	    const { data: productPool } = await db
   126	      .from('products')
   127	      .select('id, seller_id, consignment_price, title')
   128	      .eq('status', 'approved')
   129	      .eq('is_active', true)
   130	      .neq('seller_id', userId)
   131	      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
   132	      .limit(toCreate * 3);
   133	
   134	    const picked = (productPool ?? []).slice(0, toCreate);
   135	    if (picked.length === 0) {
   136	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'no_product', created: 0 }), {
   137	        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   138	      });
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    let created = 0;
   142	    for (const prod of picked) {
   143	      const orderNo = `ORD${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
   144	      const { error: insertErr } = await db.from('orders').insert({
   145	        buyer_id:   userId,
   146	        seller_id:  prod.seller_id,
   147	        product_id: prod.id,
   148	        amount:     prod.consignment_price,
   149	        status:     'pending_payment',
   150	        order_no:   orderNo,
   151	        is_rush:    true,
   152	        rush_slot_id:    earlyPeriod.isCustom ? null : earlyPeriod.id,
   153	        rush_activity_id: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? earlyPeriod.id : null,
   154	      });
   155	      if (insertErr) {
   156	        console.log(`[auto-rush-early] order_fail user=${userId} prod=${prod.id} err=${insertErr.message}`);
   157	        continue;
   158	      }
   159	      await db.from('products').update({ is_active: false }).eq('id', prod.id);
   160	      await db.from('users').update({ rush_skipped_today: false }).eq('id', userId);
   161	      created++;
   162	      console.log(`[auto-rush-early] ✅ user=${userId} 自动抢购 "${prod.title}" order=${orderNo}`);
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ created, period_id: earlyPeriod.id, source: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? 'custom' : 'default' }), {
   166	      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   167	    });
   168	
   169	  } catch (err) {
   170	    console.error('[auto-rush-early]', err);
   171	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {
   172	      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   173	    });
   174	  }
   175	});
[The command completed with exit code 0.]

Path: [/workspace/app-cj6aqssgkd8h/supabase/functions/auto-rush-order/index.ts]
Thought: cron 已停用，同步修改三处代码：
Command: str_replace
Old String: ```
// auto-rush-order: 每天 09:29 北京时间自动为所有正式商家批量创建抢购订单
//
// 业务规则：
//   - 仅工作日（周一～周五，北京时间）执行
//   - 仅处理 merchant_type='regular' 且未被封禁的正式商家
//   - 每位正式商家每天自动创建 earlyMax（默认2）单
//   - 商品来源：其他卖家的已审核、活跃商品（approved + is_active=true，seller_id != 自己）
//   - 幂等：今日已抢单数 >= earlyMax 则跳过
//   - 商品购买后标记 is_active=false，防止重复抢购
//   - 可通过 POST body { triggered_by: "manual" } 手动触发（跳过工作日校验）

import { createClient } from 'https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2';

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
};

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });

  try {
    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;
    const serviceKey  = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!;
    const db = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, {
      auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    });

    const body = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};
    const triggeredBy: string = body.triggered_by ?? 'cron';
    const forceRun = triggeredBy === 'manual'; // 手动触发时跳过工作日检查

    // ── 北京时间计算 ──
    const nowUtc = new Date();
    const bjNow  = new Date(nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000); // UTC+8
    const bjDow  = bjNow.getUTCDay(); // 0=周日, 1=周一, …, 6=周六

    // 工作日检查（手动触发时跳过）
    if (!forceRun && (bjDow === 0 || bjDow === 6)) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'weekend' }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // 今日北京日期（用于幂等检查）
    const bjTodayStr = bjNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // "YYYY-MM-DD"
    // 今日北京 0 点对应的 UTC 时间
    const bjTodayStartUtc = new Date(
      Math.floor((nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000
    ).toISOString();

    // 正式商家早场固定额度
    const earlyMax = 2;

    // ── 解析当前生效的「早场」时段：自定义活动优先 > 默认时段 ──
    const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
    const { data: activities } = await db
      .from('rush_activities')
      .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .eq('session_type', 'early')
      .eq('activity_date', bjTodayStr)
      .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    const customEarly = (activities ?? []).find(a => nowMins >= a.start_minute && nowMins < a.end_minute);

    let earlyPeriod: { id: string; isCustom: boolean } | null = null;
    if (customEarly) {
      earlyPeriod = { id: customEarly.id, isCustom: true };
    } else {
      const { data: earlySlots } = await db
        .from('rush_time_slots')
        .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .eq('session_type', 'early')
        .order('priority', { ascending: true });
      const def = (earlySlots ?? []).find(s => nowMins >= s.start_minute && nowMins < s.end_minute);
      if (def) earlyPeriod = { id: def.id, isCustom: false };
    }
    if (!earlyPeriod) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_in_early_session', bj_date: bjTodayStr }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 查询所有正式商家（未封禁、已激活） ──
    const { data: regularMerchants, error: merchantErr } = await db
      .from('users')
      .select('id, nickname, phone')
      .eq('merchant_type', 'regular')
      .eq('is_super_admin', false)
      .eq('is_banned', false)
      .eq('user_status', 'active');

    if (merchantErr) throw new Error(`查询正式商家失败: ${merchantErr.message}`);

    const merchants = regularMerchants ?? [];

    // ── 预先拉取所有可用商品池（approved + is_active=true），先进先出分配 ──
    const { data: productPool } = await db
      .from('products')
      .select('id, seller_id, consignment_price, title')
      .eq('status', 'approved')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true });

    const availablePool: { id: string; seller_id: string | null; consignment_price: number; title: string }[]
      = [...(productPool ?? [])];

    const log: string[] = [];
    let totalCreated = 0;
    let totalSkipped = 0;

    for (const merchant of merchants) {
      // ── 幂等：查今日已创建的抢购单数 ──
      const { count: todayCnt } = await db
        .from('orders')
        .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
        .eq('buyer_id', merchant.id)
        .eq('is_rush', true)
        .gte('created_at', bjTodayStartUtc);

      const alreadyHas = todayCnt ?? 0;
      const toCreate   = earlyMax - alreadyHas; // 还需创建的订单数

      if (toCreate <= 0) {
        totalSkipped++;
        log.push(`skip:${merchant.phone}(已有${alreadyHas}单)`);
        continue;
      }

      // ── 从池中取可用商品（排除自己作为 seller 的商品）──
      const picked: typeof availablePool = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < availablePool.length && picked.length < toCreate; i++) {
        if (availablePool[i].seller_id !== merchant.id) {
          picked.push(availablePool[i]);
        }
      }

      if (picked.length === 0) {
        log.push(`no_product:${merchant.phone}(商品池已空)`);
        continue;
      }

      // ── 逐个商品创建抢购订单 ──
      let created = 0;
      for (const prod of picked) {
        const orderNo = `ORD${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
        const { error: insertErr } = await db.from('orders').insert({
          buyer_id:   merchant.id,
          seller_id:  prod.seller_id,
          product_id: prod.id,
          amount:     prod.consignment_price,
          status:     'pending_payment',
          order_no:   orderNo,
          is_rush:    true,
          rush_slot_id:    earlyPeriod.isCustom ? null : earlyPeriod.id,
          rush_activity_id: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? earlyPeriod.id : null,
        });

        if (insertErr) {
          log.push(`order_fail:${merchant.phone} prod=${prod.id} err=${insertErr.message}`);
          continue;
        }

        // 标记商品不再活跃（防止重复抢购）
        await db.from('products')
          .update({ is_active: false })
          .eq('id', prod.id);

        // 从本地池中移除已分配商品（防止同批次重复分配）
        const idx = availablePool.findIndex(p => p.id === prod.id);
        if (idx !== -1) availablePool.splice(idx, 1);

        // 清除商家"未抢"标记
        await db.from('users')
          .update({ rush_skipped_today: false })
          .eq('id', merchant.id);

        created++;
        totalCreated++;
        console.log(`[auto-rush-order] ✅ ${merchant.phone} 自动抢购 "${prod.title}" 订单=${orderNo}`);
      }

      log.push(`ok:${merchant.phone}(新建${created}单)`);
    }

    const summary = {
      triggered_by: triggeredBy,
      bj_date:      bjTodayStr,
      merchants:    merchants.length,
      orders_created: totalCreated,
      skipped:      totalSkipped,
      pool_remaining: availablePool.length,
      early_max:    earlyMax,
      log,
    };

    console.log(`[auto-rush-order] 完成 | ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);

    return new Response(JSON.stringify(summary), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });

  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[auto-rush-order]', err);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});
```

```
// auto-rush-order: 【已禁用】自动批量抢单功能已关闭，系统改为纯手动抢单模式。
// 保留此函数仅为兼容旧 cron 配置，调用即返回 disabled。

const corsHeaders = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
};

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  console.log('[auto-rush-order] 自动抢单已禁用，返回 disabled');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'auto_rush_disabled' }), {
    headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  });
});
```

```
// auto-rush-early: 正式商家进入早场时，系统自动为其完成最多 2 单抢购（按需触发，幂等）

// 业务规则：
```

```
// 正式商家早市限购数量从 morning_incentive_config.regular_first_order_limit 读取（默认 2）

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });

  try {
    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!;
    const serviceKey  = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')!;
    const db = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey, {
      auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
    });

    const body = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};
    const userId: string | undefined = body.user_id;

    if (!userId) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: '缺少 user_id' }), {
        status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 北京时间计算 ──
    const nowUtc = new Date();
    const bjNow  = new Date(nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000);
    const bjDow  = bjNow.getUTCDay();
    const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
    const bjTodayStartUtc = new Date(
      Math.floor((nowUtc.getTime() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000
    ).toISOString();
    const bjTodayStr = bjNow.toISOString().slice(0, 10); // "YYYY-MM-DD"（北京时间今日）

    // 工作日检查
    if (bjDow === 0 || bjDow === 6) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'weekend', created: 0 }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 校验用户为正式商家 ──
    const { data: user, error: userErr } = await db
      .from('users')
      .select('id, merchant_type, is_banned, user_status')
      .eq('id', userId)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (userErr) throw new Error(`查询用户失败: ${userErr.message}`);
    if (!user || user.merchant_type !== 'regular' || user.is_banned || user.user_status !== 'active') {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_regular', created: 0 }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 解析当前生效的「早场」时段：自定义活动优先 > 默认时段 ──
    const { data: activities } = await db
      .from('rush_activities')
      .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .eq('session_type', 'early')
      .eq('activity_date', bjTodayStr)
      .order('priority', { ascending: true });
    const customEarly = (activities ?? []).find(a => nowMins >= a.start_minute && nowMins < a.end_minute);

    let earlyPeriod: { id: string; isCustom: boolean } | null = null;
    if (customEarly) {
      earlyPeriod = { id: customEarly.id, isCustom: true };
    } else {
      const { data: slots } = await db
        .from('rush_time_slots')
        .select('id, start_minute, end_minute')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .eq('session_type', 'early')
        .order('priority', { ascending: true });
      const def = (slots ?? []).find(s => nowMins >= s.start_minute && nowMins < s.end_minute);
      if (def) earlyPeriod = { id: def.id, isCustom: false };
    }

    if (!earlyPeriod) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'not_in_early_session', created: 0 }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 幂等：今日已抢单数 ──
    const { count: todayCnt } = await db
      .from('orders')
      .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('buyer_id', userId)
      .eq('is_rush', true)
      .gte('created_at', bjTodayStartUtc);

    const alreadyHas = todayCnt ?? 0;
    // 正式商家早市限购数量（来自早市激励配置，默认 2）
    const { data: incentiveCfg } = await db
      .from('morning_incentive_config')
      .select('regular_first_order_limit')
      .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();
    const earlyMax = incentiveCfg?.regular_first_order_limit ?? 2;
    const toCreate = Math.max(0, earlyMax - alreadyHas);
    if (toCreate <= 0) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'already_filled', created: 0 }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    // ── 从商品池取可用商品（排除自己作为 seller）──
    const { data: productPool } = await db
      .from('products')
      .select('id, seller_id, consignment_price, title')
      .eq('status', 'approved')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .neq('seller_id', userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      .limit(toCreate * 3);

    const picked = (productPool ?? []).slice(0, toCreate);
    if (picked.length === 0) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'no_product', created: 0 }), {
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    let created = 0;
    for (const prod of picked) {
      const orderNo = `ORD${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
      const { error: insertErr } = await db.from('orders').insert({
        buyer_id:   userId,
        seller_id:  prod.seller_id,
        product_id: prod.id,
        amount:     prod.consignment_price,
        status:     'pending_payment',
        order_no:   orderNo,
        is_rush:    true,
        rush_slot_id:    earlyPeriod.isCustom ? null : earlyPeriod.id,
        rush_activity_id: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? earlyPeriod.id : null,
      });
      if (insertErr) {
        console.log(`[auto-rush-early] order_fail user=${userId} prod=${prod.id} err=${insertErr.message}`);
        continue;
      }
      await db.from('products').update({ is_active: false }).eq('id', prod.id);
      await db.from('users').update({ rush_skipped_today: false }).eq('id', userId);
      created++;
      console.log(`[auto-rush-early] ✅ user=${userId} 自动抢购 "${prod.title}" order=${orderNo}`);
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ created, period_id: earlyPeriod.id, source: earlyPeriod.isCustom ? 'custom' : 'default' }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });

  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[auto-rush-early]', err);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {
      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});
```
New String: ```

  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  console.log('[auto-rush-early] 自动抢单已禁用，返回 disabled');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skipped: true, reason: 'auto_rush_disabled', created: 0 }), {

  });
});
```

    20 /**
    21  * 服务器时钟同步：以服务器（北京时间）为唯一时间依据
    22  * - serverClockOffset：服务器时间与本地时间的差值（ms）
    23  * - getServerNow()：返回校准后的当前时间戳
    24  * - syncServerClock()：通过查询数据库时间获取偏移（页面挂载时调用一次）
    25  */
    26 let serverClockOffset = 0;
    27 function getServerNow(): number {
    28   return Date.now() + serverClockOffset;
    29 }
    30 async function syncServerClock(): Promise<void> {
    31   try {
    32     const t0 = Date.now();
    33     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_server_time');
    34     const t1 = Date.now();
    35     if (error || !data) return;
    36     const serverMs = new Date(data as string).getTime();
    37     // 用请求往返中点近似网络耗时，减少误差
    38     serverClockOffset = serverMs - Math.floor((t0 + t1) / 2);
    39   } catch {
    40     // 同步失败则沿用本地时间，不影响功能
    41   }
    42 }

   103 /** 将秒数拆分为 天/时/分/秒 */
   104 function splitDuration(totalSec: number) {
   105   const d = Math.floor(totalSec / 86400);
   106   const h = Math.floor((totalSec % 86400) / 3600);
   107   const m = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
   108   const s = totalSec % 60;
   109   return { d, h, m, s };
   110 }
   111 
   112 /** 用于“下一次抢单”计算的最小时段形状（默认时段与自定义活动通用） */
   113 type NextRushSlot = Pick<RushPeriod, 'id' | 'name' | 'start_minute' | 'end_minute' | 'session_type'>;
   114 
   115 /**
   116  * 计算下一次抢单的开始时间（考虑工作日规则：周末顺延到下一个工作日）
   117  * 返回该场次的绝对时间戳（ms）、对应时段、今日是否为周末、距今天数
   118  */
   119 function computeNextRush(slots: NextRushSlot[]): { ms: number; slot: NextRushSlot; isWeekend: boolean; daysAhead: number } | null {
   120   if (slots.length === 0) return null;
   121   const sorted = slots.slice().sort((a, b) => a.start_minute - b.start_minute);
   122   const bjNow = new Date(getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000);
   123   const bjMidnightToday = Math.floor(bjNow.getTime() / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000;
   124   const todayMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
   125   const todayDow = bjNow.getUTCDay();
   126   const isWeekend = todayDow === 0 || todayDow === 6;
   127   // 今日（工作日）仍有未开始的场次
   128   if (!isWeekend) {
   129     const todayNext = sorted.find(s => s.start_minute > todayMins);
   130     if (todayNext) {
   131       return { ms: bjMidnightToday + todayNext.start_minute * 60000, slot: todayNext, isWeekend: false, daysAhead: 0 };
   132     }
   133   }
   134   // 顺延到下一个工作日（最早场次）
   135   for (let offset = 1; offset <= 7; offset++) {
   136     const dayMidnight = bjMidnightToday + offset * 86400000;
   137     const dow = new Date(dayMidnight + 8 * 3600000).getUTCDay();
   138     if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) {
   139       return { ms: dayMidnight + sorted[0].start_minute * 60000, slot: sorted[0], isWeekend, daysAhead: offset };
   140     }
   141   }
   142   return null;
   143 }

   147   const v = String(value).padStart(2, '0');
   148   return (
   149     <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">

   152         <div className={`absolute inset-0 rounded-xl blur-md scale-110 animate-pulse ${
   153           urgent ? 'bg-red-400/60' : 'bg-white/30'
   154         }`} />
   155         <div className={`relative backdrop-blur-sm font-black font-mono text-4xl leading-none w-16 h-16 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl shadow-lg text-white ${

   236 function RushRuleBanner({
   237   merchantType,
   238   sessionType,
   239   rc,
   240   dayLimit,
   241   used,
   242   slotName,
   243   source,
   244 }: {

   254   const isRegular = merchantType === 'regular';
   255   const left = Math.max(dayLimit - used, 0);
   256 
   257   let title = '';

   277     icon = <Flame size={15} className="text-orange-500 shrink-0" />;

   327    * 返回统一描述 RushPeriod（含 source 标识来源）
   328    */
   329   function resolveCurrentPeriod(nowMins: number): RushPeriod | null {
   330     // ① 自定义活动优先（同时间多活动取优先级数值最小者）
   331     const customMatch = todayActivities
   332       .filter(a => nowMins >= a.start_minute && nowMins < a.end_minute)
   333       .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority)[0];
   334     if (customMatch) {
   335       return { ...customMatch, source: 'custom' };
   336     }
   337     // ② 默认时段（重叠时以最新配置 updated_at 为准，优先级次之）
   338     const matching = timeSlots.filter(s => nowMins >= s.start_minute && nowMins < s.end_minute);
   339     if (matching.length > 0) {
   340       const def = matching.reduce((a, b) => {
   341         const ta = new Date(a.updated_at).getTime();
   342         const tb = new Date(b.updated_at).getTime();
   343         if (tb !== ta) return tb > ta ? b : a;
   344         return a.priority <= b.priority ? a : b;
   345       });
   346       return { ...def, source: 'default' };
   347     }
   348     return null;
   349   }
   350 
   351   const loadRushStats = useCallback(async () => {
   352     if (!mobileUser) return;
   353     const bjTodayStart = new Date(Math.floor((getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000).toISOString();
   354     const isRegular = mobileUser.merchant_type === 'regular';
   355     const [{ data: referredUsers }, { count: todayCnt }, rushOrdersRes, slotsRes, activitiesRes] = await Promise.all([
   356       // 当前用户推荐的所有用户
   357       supabase.from('users').select('id').eq('referrer_id', mobileUser.id),
   358       supabase.from('orders').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
   359         .eq('buyer_id', mobileUser.id).gte('created_at', bjTodayStart),
   360       // 正式商家：拉取今日抢购记录（is_rush=true）
   361       isRegular
   362         ? supabase.from('orders')
   363             .select('id,order_no,amount,status,created_at,products!orders_product_id_fkey(title,images),seller:users!seller_id(real_name,nickname,phone)')
   364             .eq('buyer_id', mobileUser.id)
   365             .eq('is_rush', true)
   366             .gte('created_at', bjTodayStart)
   367             .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
   368         : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
   369       // 加载启用的抢购时段配置（按优先级升序）—— 默认时间依据
   370       supabase.from('rush_time_slots')
   371         .select('id,name,start_minute,end_minute,price_discount,priority,is_active,session_type,updated_at')
   372         .eq('is_active', true)
   373         .order('priority', { ascending: true }),
   374       // 加载今日生效的自定义抢购活动（覆盖默认时段，优先级最高）
   375       supabase.from('rush_activities')
   376         .select('id,name,activity_date,start_minute,end_minute,price_discount,priority,is_active,session_type')
   377         .eq('is_active', true)
   378         .eq('activity_date', new Date(getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000).toISOString().slice(0, 10))
   379         .order('priority', { ascending: true }),
   380     ]);
   381     // 主场阶梯依据：被推荐人中"已完成订单销售"（作为买家有已完成订单）的人数
   382     const referredIds = (referredUsers ?? []).map((u: { id: string }) => u.id);
   383     let completedReferral = 0;
   384     if (referredIds.length > 0) {
   385       const { data: completedBuyers } = await supabase.from('orders')
   386         .select('buyer_id')
   387         .in('buyer_id', referredIds)
   388         .eq('status', 'confirmed');
   389       completedReferral = new Set((completedBuyers ?? []).map((o: { buyer_id: string }) => o.buyer_id)).size;
   390     }
   391     setReferralCount(completedReferral);
   392     setTodayOrderCount(todayCnt ?? 0);
   393     setTimeSlots((slotsRes.data as unknown as TimeSlot[]) ?? []);
   394     setTodayActivities((activitiesRes.data as unknown as RushActivity[]) ?? []);
   395     if (isRegular) {
   396       setTodayRushOrders((rushOrdersRes.data as unknown as TodayRushOrder[]) ?? []);
   397     }
   398     // 读取早市激励配置（正式/体验商家限购数量）
   399     const { data: cfg } = await supabase.from('morning_incentive_config')
   400       .select('regular_first_order_limit, trial_first_order_limit')
   401       .order('updated_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();
   402     if (cfg) {
   403       setIncentiveCfg({
   404         regular: cfg.regular_first_order_limit ?? 2,
   405         trial: cfg.trial_first_order_limit ?? 1,
   406       });
   407     }
   408   }, [mobileUser]);
   409 
   410   const load = useCallback(async () => {
   411     const prodRes = await supabase
   412       .from('products')
   413       .select('id,title,consignment_price,images,is_resell,is_active')
   414       .eq('status', 'approved')
   415       .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
   416       .limit(20);  // 展示全部商品（含已被抢购的）
   417     const prods = (prodRes.data as unknown as PreviewProduct[]) ?? [];
   418     setPreviewProducts(prods);
   419     setLoading(false);
   420   }, []);

   506   /**
   507    * 根据商家身份与场次类型计算个人可抢单数（与 getRushPhaseAndLimit 保持一致）
   508    *   - 早场(early)：体验商家 1 单；正式商家 2 单（系统自动）
   509    *   - 正式抢购(formal)：仅正式商家参与，推荐N人且被推荐人完成订单销售→N+1单，最多6单封顶
   510    *     rc 为"已完成订单销售的被推荐人数"
   511    */
   512   const computeDayLimit = (sessionType: 'early' | 'formal', rc: number) => {
   513     if (sessionType === 'early') {
   514       return mobileUser?.merchant_type === 'regular' ? incentiveCfg.regular : incentiveCfg.trial;
   515     }
   516     // rc=0：未推荐或推荐人无完成销售 → 主场0单；rc≥1：rc+1单，封顶6单
   517     return rc === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(rc + 1, 6);
   518   };

   525    *   2. 默认时段（rush_time_slots）：仅工作日（周一～周五）生效
   526    * 命中任一时段：应用其 price_discount，库存按当天实际挂单商品数动态核算；未命中：phase='none' 或 'ended'
   527    *
   528    * 老板（is_super_admin=true）：完全绕过所有时段与限额检查。
   529    */
   530   const getRushPhaseAndLimit = async (userId: string) => {
   531     const bjNow   = new Date(getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000);
   532     const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
   533     const bjTodayStart = new Date(
   534       Math.floor((getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000) / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000

   567       const stockCol = current.source === 'custom' ? 'rush_activity_id' : 'rush_slot_id';

   569         .eq(stockCol, current.id).gte('created_at', bjTodayStart);
   570       // 动态库存限额 = 当天实际挂单商品数（挂卖 + 转拍，已审核且在售）
   571       const { count: listed } = await supabase.from('products').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })

   581       timeSlots.some(s => nowMins >= s.end_minute);

   590     }

   709         {/* ── 动态规则提示（按商家身份 + 当前阶段）── */}
   710         {currentPeriod && mobileUser && !mobileUser.is_super_admin && referralCount !== null && (
   711           <RushRuleBanner
   712             merchantType={mobileUser.merchant_type as 'trial' | 'regular'}
   713             sessionType={currentPeriod.session_type}
   714             rc={referralCount ?? 0}
   715             dayLimit={computeDayLimit(currentPeriod.session_type, referralCount ?? 0)}
   716             used={todayOrderCount ?? 0}
   717             slotName={currentPeriod.name}
   718             source={currentPeriod.source}
   719           />
   720         )}
   721 
   722         {/* 库存数据不对外展示，用户仅可见商品信息与抢单按钮 */}
   723 
   724         {/* ── 正式商家今日抢购记录 ── */}

New String: ```
/** 用于"下一次抢单"计算的最小时段形状（默认时段与自定义活动通用） */
type NextRushSlot = Pick<RushPeriod, 'id' | 'name' | 'start_minute' | 'end_minute' | 'session_type'>;

/**
 * ─────────── 5 阶段状态机 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 *  off      = 周末或无时段配置（展示下次抢单提醒）
 *  preview  = 今日有场次但尚未开始（09:00～首场前：只读浏览商品）
 *  warming  = early 时段进行中（09:25～09:30：倒计时，禁止下单）
 *  trading  = formal 时段进行中（09:30～09:35：正式抢购，可下单）
 *  ended    = 今日所有场次已结束（展示下一期预告）
 * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
type RushPhase = 'off' | 'preview' | 'warming' | 'trading' | 'ended';

function computeRushPhase(
  currentPeriod: RushPeriod | null,
  timeSlots: TimeSlot[],
  todayActivities: RushActivity[],
  nowMins: number,
  isWeekend: boolean,
): RushPhase {
  if (currentPeriod) {
    return currentPeriod.session_type === 'early' ? 'warming' : 'trading';
  }
  const anyEnded =
    todayActivities.some(a => nowMins >= a.end_minute) ||
    timeSlots.some(s => nowMins >= s.end_minute);
  if (anyEnded) return 'ended';
  if (isWeekend && todayActivities.length === 0) return 'off';
  const allSlots = [...timeSlots, ...todayActivities];
  if (allSlots.some(s => s.start_minute > nowMins)) return 'preview';
  return 'off';
}

/** 从所有时段中找出 ≥ afterMinute 的首个 formal 时段开始分钟（驱动早场倒计时） */
function findFormalStart(allSlots: NextRushSlot[], afterMinute: number): number | null {
  const next = allSlots
    .filter(s => s.session_type === 'formal' && s.start_minute >= afterMinute)
    .sort((a, b) => a.start_minute - b.start_minute)[0];
  return next?.start_minute ?? null;
}
```

```
/** 早场预热倒计时横幅：显示距正式抢购开始的剩余时间（精确到秒） */
function WarmingCountdown({ targetMinute }: { targetMinute: number | null }) {
  const [sec, setSec] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const calc = () => {
      if (targetMinute === null) { setSec(0); return; }
      const bjNow = new Date(getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000);
      const nowMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
      const nowSecs = bjNow.getUTCSeconds();
      const remaining = Math.max(0, (targetMinute - nowMins) * 60 - nowSecs);
      setSec(remaining);
    };
    calc();
    const t = setInterval(calc, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [targetMinute]);

  const { m, s } = splitDuration(sec);
  const urgent = sec > 0 && sec <= 30;

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl"
      style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #f59e0b 0%, #f97316 55%, #ef4444 100%)' }}>
      <div className="absolute -top-8 -right-8 w-36 h-36 rounded-full bg-white/15 blur-2xl animate-pulse" />
      <div className="absolute -bottom-6 -left-4 w-28 h-28 rounded-full bg-yellow-300/15 blur-xl" />
      <div className="relative px-5 py-5 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Zap size={16} className="text-yellow-100" />
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white/90">🌅 早场预热中</span>
        </div>
        {targetMinute !== null ? (
          <>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white tracking-wide drop-shadow">距正式抢购还有</h2>
            <div className="flex items-end justify-center gap-2">
              <DigitBox value={m} label="分" urgent={urgent} />
              <span className="text-white/60 font-black text-2xl pb-5 animate-pulse">:</span>
              <DigitBox value={s} label="秒" urgent={urgent} />
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white tracking-wide drop-shadow">抢购即将开始</h2>
        )}
        <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full border ${
          urgent ? 'bg-red-900/40 border-red-300/50 animate-pulse' : 'bg-white/15 border-white/25'
        }`}>
          <Bell size={13} className={urgent ? 'text-red-200' : 'text-yellow-200'} />
          <span className="text-white text-xs font-semibold">
            {urgent ? '⚡ 即将开抢，请做好准备！' : '提前选好商品，开抢即可下单'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** 开市预览横幅：轻量版，展示距首场开抢的倒计时 */
function PreviewBanner({ nextRush }: { nextRush: { ms: number; slot: NextRushSlot } | null }) {
  const [sec, setSec] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const calc = () => setSec(Math.max(0, Math.floor(((nextRush?.ms ?? 0) - getServerNow()) / 1000)));
    calc();
    const t = setInterval(calc, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [nextRush?.ms]);
  if (!nextRush) return null;
  const { h, m, s } = splitDuration(sec);
  const urgent = sec > 0 && sec <= 60;
  const timeStr = `${h > 0 ? String(h).padStart(2, '0') + ':' : ''}${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  return (
    <div className={`rounded-xl px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between ${urgent ? 'bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-300/50' : 'bg-primary/5 border border-primary/20'}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
        <Eye size={15} className="text-primary shrink-0" />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{nextRush.slot.name} · 即将开抢</p>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">现在可提前浏览商品，开抢后方可下单</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={`flex items-center gap-1 font-mono font-bold text-sm shrink-0 ml-3 ${urgent ? 'text-orange-500 animate-pulse' : 'text-primary'}`}>
        <Clock size={13} />
        <span>{timeStr}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```
                      <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/20 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 border">{visibleProducts.length}件</Badge>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">仅预览，开抢后方可下单</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="columns-2 gap-3">
                    {visibleProducts.map(p => {
                      const img = Array.isArray(p.images) && p.images.length > 0 ? p.images[0] : null;
                      return (
                        <div key={p.id} className="break-inside-avoid mb-3 bg-card border border-border rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                          <div className="w-full aspect-[4/3] bg-muted overflow-hidden relative">
                            {img ? <img src={img} alt={p.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" onError={e => { (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'; }} />
                              : <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center"><Package size={28} className="text-muted-foreground" /></div>}
                            <span className={`absolute top-1.5 left-1.5 text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full leading-none ${p.is_resell ? 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-green-500 text-white'}`}>
                              {p.is_resell ? '转拍' : '寄卖'}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="px-2.5 pt-2 pb-2.5 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                            <p className="text-xs text-foreground leading-snug" style={{ display: '-webkit-box', WebkitLineClamp: 2, WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical', overflow: 'hidden' }}>{p.title}</p>
                            <p className="text-orange-500 font-bold text-sm">¥{p.consignment_price.toLocaleString()}</p>
                            <div className="w-full h-8 rounded-md bg-muted flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                              <Clock size={11} className="text-muted-foreground" />
                              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">开抢后方可下单</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-center py-10 text-muted-foreground">
                  <Package size={36} className="mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />
                  <p className="text-sm">正在准备本期商品</p>
                  <p className="text-xs mt-1">开市前将为您展示全部待售商品</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

          ) : rushPhase === 'warming' ? (
            /* ── 早场预热（09:25～09:30）：只读商品 + 禁用按钮（WarmingCountdown 已在上方显示） ── */
            visibleProducts.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
                <Package size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
                <p className="text-sm">今日商品已抢完</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Eye size={15} className="text-primary" />
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">本期抢购商品</span>
                    <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/20 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 border">{visibleProducts.length}件</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-orange-500 font-medium animate-pulse">即将开抢</span>
                </div>
                <div className="columns-2 gap-3">
                  {visibleProducts.map(p => {
                    const img = Array.isArray(p.images) && p.images.length > 0 ? p.images[0] : null;
                    return (
                      <div key={p.id} className="break-inside-avoid mb-3 bg-card border border-border rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                        <div className="w-full aspect-[4/3] bg-muted overflow-hidden relative">
                          {img ? <img src={img} alt={p.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" onError={e => { (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'; }} />
                            : <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center"><Package size={28} className="text-muted-foreground" /></div>}
                          <span className={`absolute top-1.5 left-1.5 text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full leading-none ${p.is_resell ? 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-green-500 text-white'}`}>
                            {p.is_resell ? '转拍' : '寄卖'}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="px-2.5 pt-2 pb-2.5 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                          <p className="text-xs text-foreground leading-snug" style={{ display: '-webkit-box', WebkitLineClamp: 2, WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical', overflow: 'hidden' }}>{p.title}</p>
                          <p className="text-orange-500 font-bold text-sm">¥{p.consignment_price.toLocaleString()}</p>
                          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="w-full h-8 text-xs gap-1" disabled>
                            <Clock size={11} />即将开抢
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )

          ) : /* trading */ visibleProducts.length === 0 ? (
            /* ── 正式抢购 · 已抢完 ── */
            <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
              <Package size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
              <p className="text-sm">今日商品已抢完</p>
              <p className="text-xs mt-1">已售出的商品已自动移除</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* ── 正式抢购（09:30～09:35）：可下单商品瀑布流 ── */
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ShoppingBag size={15} className="text-orange-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">今日抢购商品</span>
                  <Badge className="bg-orange-500 text-white text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 border-0">{visibleProducts.length}件</Badge>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-orange-500 font-medium animate-pulse">{currentPeriod!.name} 抢购中</span>
              </div>
              <div className="columns-2 gap-3">
                {visibleProducts.map(p => {
                  const img = Array.isArray(p.images) && p.images.length > 0 ? p.images[0] : null;
                  const isRushingThis = rushingProd === p.id;
                  return (
                    <div key={p.id} className="break-inside-avoid mb-3 bg-card border border-border rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="w-full aspect-[4/3] bg-muted overflow-hidden relative cursor-pointer active:opacity-90" onClick={() => handleFlashBuy(p)}>
                        {img ? (
                          <img src={img} alt={p.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" onError={e => { (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'; }} />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                            <Package size={28} className="text-muted-foreground" />
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <span className={`absolute top-1.5 left-1.5 text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full leading-none ${p.is_resell ? 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-green-500 text-white'}`}>
                          {p.is_resell ? '转拍' : '寄卖'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="px-2.5 pt-2 pb-2.5 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                        <p className="text-xs text-foreground leading-snug" style={{ display: '-webkit-box', WebkitLineClamp: 2, WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical', overflow: 'hidden' }}>{p.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-orange-500 font-bold text-sm">¥{p.consignment_price.toLocaleString()}</p>
                        <Button size="sm"
                          className="w-full h-8 text-xs bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600 text-white border-0 gap-1"
                          disabled={isRushingThis}
                          onClick={() => handleFlashBuy(p)}>
                          {isRushingThis
                            ? <><span className="animate-spin inline-block">⏳</span>抢购中…</>
                            : <><Zap size={11} />立即抢购</>}
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
```

   155 function findFormalStart(allSlots: NextRushSlot[], afterMinute: number): number | null {
   156   const next = allSlots
   157     .filter(s => s.session_type === 'formal' && s.start_minute >= afterMinute)
   158     .sort((a, b) => a.start_minute - b.start_minute)[0];
   159   return next?.start_minute ?? null;
   160 }
   161 
   162 /**
   163  * 计算下一次抢单的开始时间（考虑工作日规则：周末顺延到下一个工作日）
   164  * 返回该场次的绝对时间戳（ms）、对应时段、今日是否为周末、距今天数
   165  */
   166 function computeNextRush(slots: NextRushSlot[]): { ms: number; slot: NextRushSlot; isWeekend: boolean; daysAhead: number } | null {
   167   if (slots.length === 0) return null;
   168   const sorted = slots.slice().sort((a, b) => a.start_minute - b.start_minute);
   169   const bjNow = new Date(getServerNow() + 8 * 3600000);
   170   const bjMidnightToday = Math.floor(bjNow.getTime() / 86400000) * 86400000 - 8 * 3600000;
   171   const todayMins = bjNow.getUTCHours() * 60 + bjNow.getUTCMinutes();
   172   const todayDow = bjNow.getUTCDay();
   173   const isWeekend = todayDow === 0 || todayDow === 6;
   174   // 今日（工作日）仍有未开始的场次
   175   if (!isWeekend) {
   176     const todayNext = sorted.find(s => s.start_minute > todayMins);
   177     if (todayNext) {
   178       return { ms: bjMidnightToday + todayNext.start_minute * 60000, slot: todayNext, isWeekend: false, daysAhead: 0 };
   179     }
   180   }
   181   // 顺延到下一个工作日（最早场次）
   182   for (let offset = 1; offset <= 7; offset++) {
   183     const dayMidnight = bjMidnightToday + offset * 86400000;
   184     const dow = new Date(dayMidnight + 8 * 3600000).getUTCDay();
   185     if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) {
   186       return { ms: dayMidnight + sorted[0].start_minute * 60000, slot: sorted[0], isWeekend, daysAhead: offset };
   187     }
   188   }
   189   return null;
   190 }
   191

   193 function DigitBox({ value, label, urgent }: { value: number; label: string; urgent?: boolean }) {
   194   const v = String(value).padStart(2, '0');
   195   return (
